# Graph Report - Sterling  (2026-07-12)

## Corpus Check
- 2891 files · ~3,414,996 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.

## Summary
- 36193 nodes · 55305 edges · 3213 communities (2834 shown, 379 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 92% EXTRACTED · 8% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 4480 edges (avg confidence: 0.7)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `c1e7f82a`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
